 <project name="CNC" default="package" basedir=".">
  <property name="src" value="src/main/java"/>
  <property name="build" value="build"/>
  <property name="dist" value="dist"/>
  
  <target name="init">
    <mkdir dir="${build}"/>
    <mkdir dir="${dist}"/>
  </target>
  
  <target name="compile" depends="init">
      <javac srcdir="${src}" destdir="${build}" includeantruntime="false" debug="on" source="17" target="17">
      <classpath>
        <pathelement location="weka.jar"/>
      </classpath>
    </javac>
  </target>
  
  <target name="package" depends="compile">
    <jar destfile="${dist}/CNC.jar" basedir="${build}"/>
    <copy todir="${dist}">
      <fileset dir="${basedir}">
        <include name="Description.props"/>
      </fileset>
    </copy>
    <zip destfile="${dist}/CNC.zip" basedir="${dist}"/>
  </target>

  <target name="clean">
    <delete dir="${build}"/>
    <delete dir="${dist}"/>
  </target>
</project>
